   347	
   348	def pi_auth_provider_keys() -> frozenset[str]:
   349	    """Read-only key-SET of `~/.pi/agent/auth.json` (spike Q2): provider NAMES only, never
   350	    token values, never logged. Any surprise (missing file, symlink, foreign-owned file,
   351	    foreign shape, bad JSON) is an empty set, fail-closed — this never raises and the
   352	    caller never sees a credential value, only whether a provider name is present as a
   353	    key. P3-SEC-002 repair (022 PKG-3): the uid check was missing here even though
   354	    `_read_credential_json` (below) has always had it -- a file owned by another uid on
   355	    the same box could be read and its keys folded into a routing decision (the real
   356	    gate at `_probe_pairs`, `provider not in pi_auth_provider_keys()`) as if it were this
   357	    user's own credential state. Same discipline as `_read_credential_json` now: `lstat`,
   358	    never a symlink, a regular file, owned by THIS uid."""
   359	    path = Path.home() / ".pi/agent/auth.json"
   360	    try:
   361	        st = path.lstat()
   362	        if _stat.S_ISLNK(st.st_mode) or not _stat.S_ISREG(st.st_mode) or st.st_uid != os.getuid():
   363	            return frozenset()
   364	        doc = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
   365	    except (OSError, ValueError):
   366	        return frozenset()
   367	    if not isinstance(doc, dict):
   368	        return frozenset()
   369	    return frozenset(key for key in doc if isinstance(key, str))
   370	
   371	
   372	def _binary_signature(name: str) -> str:
   373	    """ADR-0034 (AC-08), generalized by ADR-0043 (022 PKG-3, AC-07): path + mtime of
   374	    `name`'s resolved binary on PATH right now -- swapping the binary (upgrade,
   375	    reinstall, a different shim) invalidates every existing cache entry without a
   376	    manual version bump. Originally scoped to `opencode` alone; AC-07 extends the same
   377	    argument to `codex` and `claude` too -- any of the three CLI binaries can change how
   378	    it reads/writes its OWN credential store on a swap, so each binary's signature is
   379	    folded into `_cache_key` alongside its runtime's credential-file signature (below).
   380	    No subprocess here, just a PATH lookup and a stat -- safe to call from a pure
   381	    hashing function. Unresolvable is a fixed per-name sentinel that simply never
   382	    equals a real signature, so the cache is skipped (fail-closed), never fabricated as
   383	    a match."""
   384	    path = shutil.which(name)
   385	    if not path:
   386	        return f"{name}:absent"
   387	    try:
   388	        mtime_ns = os.stat(path).st_mtime_ns
   389	    except OSError:
   390	        return f"{name}:absent"
   391	    return f"{name}:{path}:{mtime_ns}"
   392	
   393	
   394	# ------------------------------------------------------------------ ADR-0043 (022 PKG-3)
   395	# Per-runtime credential signatures, AC-07: closes "de-auth invisible for up to
   396	# PROBE_CACHE_TTL (300s) in 3 of 4 runtimes" -- `_cache_key` used to fold in ONLY
   397	# opencode's live subprocess-based auth signature (`_live_opencode_auth_signature`
   398	# above). Every signature below is `stat`/local-read only -- ZERO new subprocesses
   399	# (the one subprocess this whole cache-key computation ever pays stays
   400	# `_live_opencode_auth_signature`, called by the CALLER and passed in, never invoked
   401	# from here -- see `test_adr0043_ac07_cache_key_never_spawns_a_subprocess_for_the_new_
   402	# signatures` in tests/test_routing.py and evidence/P3-implementer.md's proof). Same
   403	# security discipline as `pi_auth_provider_keys` throughout (AC-09): `lstat` (never a
   404	# symlink), a regular file, this uid, ONLY named fields ever read -- never a whole-file
   405	# hash or mtime, because `~/.claude/.credentials.json` also holds an unrelated
   406	# `mcpOAuth` block (today a Vercel token, live-measured) that rotates on ITS OWN MCP
   407	# refresh cycle with zero relation to the Claude Code provider credential; hashing the
   408	# file or its mtime would rotate this signature on every MCP token refresh, defeating
   409	# the cache for a reason that has nothing to do with what this signature exists to
   410	# track. Every value is hashed before being folded into `_cache_key` -- never logged,
   411	# never placed in an envelope, never a raw credential value.
   412	
   413	
   414	def _read_credential_json(path: Path) -> dict | None:
   415	    """Shared fail-closed read for a local per-runtime credential file: `lstat` (never
   416	    a symlink), a regular file, owned by this uid, valid JSON, a dict -- ANY surprise is
   417	    `None`, never raised, same discipline as `pi_auth_provider_keys` above. The caller
   418	    reads only specific NAMED fields from the result; this function itself never
   419	    inspects field values, so widening what a signature reads is always a one-line,
   420	    auditable change at the call site, never here."""
   421	    try:
   422	        st = path.lstat()
   423	        if _stat.S_ISLNK(st.st_mode) or not _stat.S_ISREG(st.st_mode) or st.st_uid != os.getuid():
   424	            return None
   425	        doc = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
   426	    except (OSError, ValueError):
   427	        return None
   428	    return doc if isinstance(doc, dict) else None
   429	
   430	
   431	def _codex_auth_signature() -> str:
   432	    """AC-07/AC-09: codex's per-runtime signature -- `~/.codex/auth.json`, ONLY
   433	    `tokens.account_id` (real account identity; codex is the one runtime of the three
   434	    whose credential file carries one, ADR-0043) and whether `OPENAI_API_KEY` is
   435	    present (a boolean, never the key's own value). NEVER `tokens.access_token`/
   436	    `tokens.refresh_token`/`tokens.id_token`/`last_refresh` -- token material and its
   437	    timestamp, which rotate on every refresh (AC-08 property 1: a signature built from
   438	    a rotating field would miss the cache on every routine OAuth renewal). File absent,
   439	    foreign-owned, a symlink, or unreadable -- fail-closed to the empty string, which
   440	    never equals a real signature, so the cache is simply skipped (a fresh probe runs),
   441	    never fabricated as a match (AC-08 property 2: this is what makes a real logout/
   442	    delete visible immediately).
   443	
   444	    P3-SEC-001 repair (022 PKG-3, AC-09): a VALID JSON object that is missing `tokens`
   445	    or whose `tokens.account_id` isn't a string (corrupt file, foreign shape, a `{}`
   446	    written mid-write) must fail closed too, exactly like a missing/foreign-owned/
   447	    unreadable file already does via `_read_credential_json` -- shape and type of every
   448	    field this signature actually reads are validated BEFORE hashing, never after. Before
   449	    this fix, a missing/mistyped `tokens.account_id` silently defaulted to `""` and STILL
   450	    got folded into a hash (`account_id=|api_key_present=...`), producing a fixed
   451	    non-empty signature instead of the required empty one -- fail-open, because that
   452	    non-empty value looked exactly like a real (if account-id-less) signature to
   453	    `_cache_key`, so a corrupt-shaped file could still warm/serve a cache entry."""
   454	    doc = _read_credential_json(Path.home() / ".codex/auth.json")
   455	    if doc is None:
   456	        return ""
   457	    tokens = doc.get("tokens")
   458	    if not isinstance(tokens, dict):
   459	        return ""
   460	    account_id = tokens.get("account_id")
   461	    if not isinstance(account_id, str):
   462	        return ""
   463	    has_api_key = isinstance(doc.get("OPENAI_API_KEY"), str)
   464	    material = f"account_id={account_id}|api_key_present={has_api_key}"
   465	    return hashlib.sha256(material.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
   466	
   467	
   468	def _claude_code_auth_signature() -> str:
   469	    """AC-07/AC-09: claude-code's per-runtime signature -- `~/.claude/.credentials.json`,
   470	    ONLY the three `claudeAiOauth` fields measured NON-rotating across a refresh
   471	    (`scopes`, `subscriptionType`, `rateLimitTier` -- attributes of the OAuth grant/plan
   472	    itself, not of the token instance; evidence/P3-implementer.md). NEVER
   473	    `accessToken`/`refreshToken`/`expiresAt`/`refreshTokenExpiresAt` (token material,
   474	    AC-09: "hashear el refreshToken no se hace"), and NEVER the file's OTHER top-level
   475	    key `mcpOAuth` (the trap this module's own section docstring names).
   476	    ADR-0043's declared limit: `claudeAiOauth` carries NO account-identity field (unlike
   477	    codex's `tokens.account_id`) -- this signature therefore detects PRESENCE/ABSENCE
   478	    (logout, deletion, file absent) but never a same-plan account swap without an
   479	    intervening logout. Closing that gap would mean hashing `refreshToken`, which this
   480	    package explicitly does not do.
   481	
   482	    P3-SEC-001 repair (022 PKG-3, AC-09): `claudeAiOauth` being present as a dict is not
   483	    enough -- shape and type of the three fields this signature actually reads are
   484	    validated BEFORE hashing (`scopes` a list, `subscriptionType`/`rateLimitTier` a
   485	    string each); any absence or wrong type fails closed to the empty string. Before this
   486	    fix, `{"claudeAiOauth": {}}` (or any object missing one of the three fields) still
   487	    produced a fixed non-empty hash of the literal string `"None"` in place of each
   488	    missing field, indistinguishable from a real signature to `_cache_key` -- fail-open
   489	    on a corrupt/foreign-shaped file."""
   490	    doc = _read_credential_json(Path.home() / ".claude/.credentials.json")
   491	    if doc is None:
   492	        return ""
   493	    oauth = doc.get("claudeAiOauth")
   494	    if not isinstance(oauth, dict):
   495	        return ""
   496	    scopes, subscription_type, rate_limit_tier = (oauth.get(f) for f in
   497	                                                   ("scopes", "subscriptionType", "rateLimitTier"))
   498	    if not isinstance(scopes, list) or not isinstance(subscription_type, str) \
   499	            or not isinstance(rate_limit_tier, str):
   500	        return ""
   501	    parts = [str(scopes), subscription_type, rate_limit_tier]
   502	    return hashlib.sha256("|".join(parts).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
   503	
   504	
   505	def _pi_auth_signature() -> str:
   506	    """AC-07: pi's per-runtime signature folds `pi_auth_provider_keys()` (already
   507	    stat/local-read only, zero subprocess, spike Q2 discipline -- untouched by this
   508	    package beyond the uid check, P3-SEC-002) with `PI_PINNED_VERSION` (the EXACT pi
   509	    release the probe invokes, above) -- a version bump is itself credential-relevant: a
   510	    different pinned pi release could read/write a differently-shaped auth.json. Provider
   511	    NAMES only (never a token value), hashed before folding into `_cache_key`.
   512	
   513	    P3-SEC-002 repair (022 PKG-3, AC-09): an EMPTY key-set (file absent, symlink,
   514	    foreign-owned, corrupt/foreign-shaped JSON -- anything `pi_auth_provider_keys()`
   515	    itself already fails closed on) must fail closed HERE too, to the empty string, same
   516	    as `_codex_auth_signature`/`_claude_code_auth_signature` above. Before this fix, an
   517	    empty key-set was folded into a hash alongside `PI_PINNED_VERSION` regardless,
   518	    producing a fixed non-empty signature for "pi has no credentials" -- fail-OPEN,
   519	    because a non-empty signature is indistinguishable, at the `_cache_key` level, from a
   520	    real credential state. This is safe to change: `_pi_auth_signature`'s only consumer
   521	    is `_cache_key` (cache freshness), never the real per-pair authentication gate
   522	    (`_probe_pairs` calls `pi_auth_provider_keys()` directly, catalog.py:753) -- an empty
   523	    signature can only ever cost a cache miss (a fresh, correctly-gated probe runs next),
   524	    never fabricate or suppress which pairs are actually authenticated."""
   525	    keys = pi_auth_provider_keys()
   526	    if not keys:
   527	        return ""
   528	    names = ",".join(sorted(keys))
   529	    return hashlib.sha256(f"{names}|{PI_PINNED_VERSION}".encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
   530	
   531	
   532	def _live_opencode_auth_signature(timeout: float) -> str:
   533	    """ADR-0034 (AC-08): a cheap, ALWAYS-fresh (never itself cached) read of `opencode
   534	    auth list --pure` -- credential NAMES only, never a model listing, never persisted
   535	    to disk on its own -- folded into the cache key so the authenticated-provider set is
   536	    genuinely re-checked on every composition that consults the cache, instead of trusted
   537	    from whatever it was when the cache was last written. Any surprise (missing binary,
   538	    timeout, non-zero exit, parse failure) is the empty signature, fail-closed: an
   539	    unreadable auth state simply differs from a real one, so the cache is missed and a
   540	    full fresh probe runs -- it can never fabricate a match."""
   541	    probe_env = dict(os.environ, CI="1", NO_COLOR="1", TERM="dumb")
   542	    try:
   543	        completed = subprocess.run(("opencode", "auth", "list", "--pure"), stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
   544	                                   stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, text=True,
   545	                                   timeout=timeout, check=False, env=probe_env)
   546	    except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
   547	        return ""
   548	    if completed.returncode != 0:
   549	        return ""
   550	    try:
   551	        return ",".join(sorted(_parse_opencode_auth(completed.stdout)))
   552	    except RoutingError:
   553	        return ""
   554	
   555	
   556	def _cache_key(config: dict, auth_signature: str = "") -> str:
   557	    """ADR-0006 base key (uid + canonical catalog/routing config), the cache schema
   558	    version, always; extended by ADR-0034 AC-08 with the resolved `opencode` binary's
   559	    path+mtime, and by ADR-0043 (022 PKG-3, AC-07) with EVERY runtime's own
   560	    stat/local-read-only credential signature (`_codex_auth_signature`,
   561	    `_claude_code_auth_signature`, `_pi_auth_signature`) plus the `codex`/`claude`
   562	    binary signatures (`_binary_signature`, generalized from the opencode-only one) --
   563	    none of these six calls does any I/O beyond a stat/local read, so this function
   564	    stays pure/no-subprocess for every existing direct caller (proven by
   565	    `test_adr0043_ac07_cache_key_never_spawns_a_subprocess_for_the_new_signatures`,
   566	    tests/test_routing.py). `auth_signature` -- the caller-supplied, freshly-read,
   567	    normalized opencode-authenticated-provider set, the ONE genuinely subprocess-backed
   568	    signal in this whole key -- is folded in only when the caller actually has one.
   569	    `probe_inventory` is the only production caller that supplies a non-empty
   570	    `auth_signature` (see `_live_opencode_auth_signature` above); every pre-existing
   571	    direct caller (including tests) keeps calling this with the same two-argument shape
   572	    it always had and is unaffected. The ENTIRE key changes when ANY one runtime's
   573	    credential state changes -- the probe-cache document covers every runtime's pairs
   574	    at once, so a credential change in one runtime correctly forces a fresh probe of
   575	    ALL pairs on the next decision, never a partial/stale mix."""
   576	    canon = json.dumps({"catalog": config.get("catalog", {}), "routing": config.get("routing", {})},
   577	                       sort_keys=True, default=str)
   578	    parts = "\n".join((str(os.getuid()), canon, str(_CACHE_SCHEMA_VERSION),
   579	                       _binary_signature("opencode"), _binary_signature("codex"), _binary_signature("claude"),
   580	                       _codex_auth_signature(), _claude_code_auth_signature(), _pi_auth_signature(),
   581	                       auth_signature))
   582	    return hashlib.sha256(parts.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
   583	
   584	
   585	def _validate_cache_dir(root: Path) -> bool:
   586	    """Same private-directory discipline as the store: no symlink, this uid's 0700 dir.
   587	
   588	    Never creates or chmods anything; an unvalidated root just means the cache
   589	    is skipped (read AND write) and every pair is probed fresh (SEC-A03/F06).
   590	    """
   591	    try:
   592	        st = root.lstat()
   593	    except OSError:
   594	        return False
   595	    return (_stat.S_ISDIR(st.st_mode) and not _stat.S_ISLNK(st.st_mode)
   596	            and st.st_uid == os.getuid() and _stat.S_IMODE(st.st_mode) == 0o700)
   597	
   598	
   599	def _read_probe_cache(cache_root, key: str, now: float, config: dict):
   600	    """Valid cache or None; anything unexpected is ignored fail-closed (fresh probes run).
   601	
   602	    Only PAIRS PRESENT are ever positive (F06: negatives are never persisted,
   603	    so a transient failure costs one retry, never the full TTL). Every entry
   604	    is re-checked against the live `resolve_ceiling` state on read (F09), so a
   605	    stale-but-key-matching cache can never widen the audited set. ADR-0042 (022 PKG-2):
   606	    the three tri-states matter here as much as at `_probe_pairs` -- a "veto" provider
   607	    is dropped outright (never served from cache, however it got written); a "curated"
   608	    provider is re-intersected exactly like before; an "auto" provider has NO ceiling to
   609	    intersect against, so the cached models are kept as-is. Naively re-intersecting
   610	    against `set()` in "auto" mode (the pre-tri-state behavior applied blindly) would
   611	    leave every auto-mode pair's cached entry EMPTY on every read, defeating the cache
   612	    entirely -- the caller would re-probe on every single decision (up to
   613	    `PI_PROBE_MIN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS` for pi). See `test_read_probe_cache_auto_mode_is_not_
   614	    always_empty` in tests/test_routing.py, which exists specifically to catch that
   615	    regression.
   616	    """
   617	    root = Path(cache_root)
   618	    if not _validate_cache_dir(root):
   619	        return None
   620	    try:
   621	        path = root / "probe-cache.json"
   622	        st = path.lstat()
   623	        if _stat.S_ISLNK(st.st_mode) or not _stat.S_ISREG(st.st_mode) or st.st_uid != os.getuid() or _stat.S_IMODE(st.st_mode) != 0o600:
   624	            return None
   625	        doc = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
   626	        if (not isinstance(doc, dict) or doc.get("key") != key
   627	                or not isinstance(doc.get("at"), (int, float)) or isinstance(doc.get("at"), bool)
   628	                or doc["at"] > now or now - doc["at"] > PROBE_CACHE_TTL
   629	                or not isinstance(doc.get("pairs"), dict)):
   630	            return None
   631	        out: dict[tuple[str, str], set[str]] = {}
   632	        for name, models in doc["pairs"].items():
   633	            runtime, _, provider = str(name).partition("|")
   634	            if (runtime, provider) not in _PAIR_COMMANDS or not isinstance(models, list) or not all(isinstance(m, str) for m in models):
   635	                return None
   636	            state, ceiling = resolve_ceiling(config, provider)
   637	            if state == "veto":
   638	                continue  # never served from cache, no matter what is on disk (AC-05 layer 2)
   639	            # "auto": no ceiling exists to re-intersect against -- keeping `set()` here
   640	            # (the naive port of the pre-tri-state one-liner) would empty every auto-mode
   641	            # entry on every single read, defeating the cache (see docstring above).
   642	            kept = set(models) if state == "auto" else (set(models) & ceiling)
   643	            if kept:  # empty after filtering ⇒ treated as absent, retried like any negative
   644	                out[(runtime, provider)] = kept
   645	        return out
   646	    except (OSError, ValueError):
   647	        return None
   648	
   649	
   650	def _write_probe_cache(cache_root, key: str, result: dict, now: float) -> None:
   651	    """Atomic 0600 tmp+rename under the managed root; content is redacted pair->models only."""
   652	    root = Path(cache_root)
   653	    if not _validate_cache_dir(root):
   654	        return  # the store owns root creation; never create or chmod it from here
   655	    tmp = root / f".probe-cache.{os.getpid()}.tmp"
   656	    doc = {"key": key, "at": now,
   657	           "pairs": {f"{runtime}|{provider}": sorted(models) for (runtime, provider), models in result.items()}}
   658	    old = os.umask(0o077)
   659	    try:
   660	        tmp.write_text(json.dumps(doc, sort_keys=True), encoding="utf-8")
   661	        os.chmod(tmp, 0o600)
   662	        os.replace(tmp, root / "probe-cache.json")
   663	    except OSError:
   664	        try: tmp.unlink()
   665	        except OSError: pass
   666	    finally:
   667	        os.umask(old)
   668	
   669	
   670	def prune_legacy_probe_cache(legacy_root) -> bool:
   671	    """AC-10 (022 PKG-3): a single probe cache lives at the store's cache root now
   672	    (`RoutingStore.ensure_cache_root()`, `~/.local/state/set-agentes/routing-v2`) --
   673	    this removes the STALE SIBLING file at the old, divergent location
   674	    (`STATE_DIR/probe-cache.json`, what `--route-doctor`/`--doctor-all`/the 'Estado
   675	    general' panel used to read while the decision path already read/wrote the
   676	    routing-v2 one -- two caches confirmed diverging live, ADR-0043).
   677	
   678	    Same security discipline as `_write_probe_cache`, mirrored exactly: the directory
   679	    must pass `_validate_cache_dir` (0700, this uid, no symlink) and the file itself
   680	    must independently be a regular, this-uid file (never following a symlink) before
   681	    it is ever unlinked -- any surprise leaves it untouched (fail-closed: an
   682	    unrecognized foreign object is never deleted). `STATE_DIR` also holds
   683	    `config.toml`/`model-preference.toml` and other real state -- this function NEVER
   684	    touches the directory itself or any other file in it, only this one legacy
   685	    `probe-cache.json`. Returns True only when a file was actually removed (for the
   686	    caller's own best-effort bookkeeping); False for "nothing to prune" and every
   687	    fail-closed branch alike -- the caller never needs to distinguish them, pruning is
   688	    always best-effort."""
   689	    root = Path(legacy_root)
   690	    if not _validate_cache_dir(root):
   691	        return False
   692	    path = root / "probe-cache.json"
   693	    try:
   694	        st = path.lstat()
   695	    except OSError:
   696	        return False
   697	    if _stat.S_ISLNK(st.st_mode) or not _stat.S_ISREG(st.st_mode) or st.st_uid != os.getuid():
   698	        return False
   699	    try:
   700	        path.unlink()
   701	    except OSError:
   702	        return False
   703	    return True
   704	
   705	
   706	def _run_cached(ran, argv, timeout, env):
   707	    """Subprocess run for `argv`, memoized in the caller's `ran` dict (shared across pairs
   708	    in one probe call, e.g. the single `opencode auth list --pure` all opencode pairs
   709	    reuse). Returns the CompletedProcess, or None on any OSError/timeout — never raises."""
   710	    if argv not in ran:
   711	        try:
   712	            ran[argv] = subprocess.run(argv, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
   713	                                       text=True, timeout=timeout, check=False, env=env)
   714	        except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
   715	            ran[argv] = None
   716	    return ran[argv]
   717	
   718	
   719	def _probe_pairs(config: dict, pairs, timeout: float, ran=None) -> dict[tuple[str, str], set[str]]:
   720	    """Fresh, uncached, pair-scoped subprocess observation for exactly `pairs`.
   721	
   722	    Any surprise — missing binary, timeout, nonzero exit, unexpected shape,
   723	    error text — makes only that pair unavailable (never raises). A pair
   724	    without any resulting model is simply absent from the returned dict:
   725	    negative results are never a first-class cached value (F06).
   726	    """
   727	    result: dict[tuple[str, str], set[str]] = {}
   728	    # Identical argvs across pairs (e.g. `opencode auth list` for both opencode pairs) run once.
   729	    ran = {} if ran is None else ran
   730	    # Non-interactive probe environment: opencode's TUI writer blocks forever on a
   731	    # non-TTY stdout unless CI/TERM signal a dumb terminal (observed 2026-07-26).
   732	    probe_env = dict(os.environ, CI="1", NO_COLOR="1", TERM="dumb")
   733	    for pair in pairs:
   734	        runtime, provider = pair
   735	        commands = _PAIR_COMMANDS[pair]
   736	        # ADR-0042 (022 PKG-2, AC-04): "veto" is dropped before any subprocess runs at
   737	        # all -- same early-exit position the pre-tri-state `if not allowed: continue`
   738	        # had, so a curated-empty provider still costs nothing to probe. "auto" (no
   739	        # `[catalog]` key for this provider) used to fall into that SAME branch (an empty
   740	        # `set()` is falsy too) and get skipped just the same -- exactly the defect this
   741	        # package removes: `allowed` being `None` below is deliberately NOT treated as
   742	        # falsy-skip; only genuine credential/parse failure further down drops the pair.
   743	        state, allowed = resolve_ceiling(config, provider)
   744	        if state == "veto":
   745	            continue
   746	        # PKG-N02: only the pi pairs get the cold-pnpm floor; every other pair keeps the
   747	        # caller's own timeout unchanged.
   748	        pair_timeout = max(timeout, PI_PROBE_MIN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS) if runtime == "pi" else timeout
   749	        if runtime == "opencode":
   750	            # 012 AC-02: the credential-set check (map 1) and the models-listing CLI
   751	            # id/prefix (map 2) are two independently addressable lookups, never one.
   752	            # Repair F-06 (012 repair, medium, +69%/+10.5s measured without this fix):
   753	            # map 1's membership check runs BEFORE the second, more expensive `opencode
   754	            # models <id>` call, not after both commands already ran — a machine missing
   755	            # a given OpenCode-lane subscription now never pays that pair's models-call
   756	            # latency at all, instead of always paying it only to discard the result.
   757	            auth_result = _run_cached(ran, commands[0], pair_timeout, probe_env)
   758	            if auth_result is None or auth_result.returncode != 0:
   759	                continue
   760	            try:
   761	                provider_key = _OPENCODE_PROVIDER_KEYS[provider]
   762	                credentials = _parse_opencode_auth(auth_result.stdout)
   763	            except (RoutingError, ValueError, KeyError, IndexError, TypeError):
   764	                continue
   765	            if provider_key not in credentials:
   766	                continue
   767	            models_result = _run_cached(ran, commands[1], pair_timeout, probe_env)
   768	            if models_result is None or models_result.returncode != 0:
   769	                continue
   770	            try:
   771	                cli_id = _OPENCODE_CLI_IDS[provider]
   772	                parsed = _parse_opencode_models(models_result.stdout, cli_id)
   773	            except (RoutingError, ValueError, KeyError, IndexError, TypeError):
   774	                continue
   775	            # AC-05 layer 3 is unaffected by "auto": billing_rank's fail-closed-to-
   776	            # expensive default already applies to any provider PROVIDER_BILLING_KIND
   777	            # does not classify as "subscription" -- ceiling state never enters that map.
   778	            models = parsed if state == "auto" else (allowed & parsed)
   779	        else:
   780	            completed, failed = [], False
   781	            for argv in commands:
   782	                completed_item = _run_cached(ran, argv, pair_timeout, probe_env)
   783	                if completed_item is None:
   784	                    failed = True; break
   785	                completed.append(completed_item)
   786	            if failed or any(item.returncode != 0 for item in completed):
   787	                continue
   788	            try:
   789	                if runtime == "codex":
   790	                    # ADR-0042 AC-04: codex/claude-code never see state == "auto" here --
   791	                    # `models_config.load_config` dies if [catalog].codex/.claude are
   792	                    # absent or empty, so `allowed` is always the curated set by the time
   793	                    # a validated config reaches this function (see resolve_ceiling's
   794	                    # own docstring). Neither runtime has a models-LISTING command to
   795	                    # fall back on if it ever were "auto" (only a login-status boolean).
   796	                    models = allowed if _parse_codex_login(completed[0].stdout, completed[0].stderr) else set()
   797	                elif runtime == "claude-code":
   798	                    models = allowed if _parse_claude_auth(completed[0].stdout) else set()
   799	                else:  # pi
   800	                    # Belt-and-suspenders (T-305, spike Q2): the auth.json key-set is a cheap,
   801	                    # non-subprocess signal alongside the naturally fail-closed column-parse
   802	                    # below (an unauthenticated provider simply has no rows in --list-models).
   803	                    if provider not in pi_auth_provider_keys():
   804	                        continue
   805	                    # Same guarantee as codex/claude-code above: pi's two pairs are both
   806	                    # openai-codex/anthropic, always curated by validation.
   807	                    models = allowed & _parse_pi_models(completed[0].stdout, provider)
   808	            except (RoutingError, ValueError, KeyError, IndexError, TypeError):
   809	                continue
   810	        if models:
   811	            result[pair] = models
   812	    return result
   813	
   814	
   815	def probe_inventory(config: dict, timeout: float = 20.0, cache_root=None, fresh: bool = False,
   816	                    pairs=None, now=None, cache_write: bool = True) -> dict[tuple[str, str], set[str]]:
   817	    """Fresh exact pair-scoped observation with per-pair parsers.
   818	
   819	    Any surprise — missing binary, timeout, nonzero exit, unexpected shape,
   820	    error text — makes only that pair unavailable. Model names are always the
   821	    intersection with the canonical models.toml catalog for the provider, so a
   822	    runtime can never widen the audited model set. Raw provider output
   823	    (including account identifiers) is parsed and discarded, never returned.
   824	
   825	    `pairs` restricts probing to those exact pairs, always fresh and never
   826	    cached (the AM-2 fresh-selected path). Otherwise, with `cache_root`, a
   827	    valid unexpired cache (ADR-0006 key) filters candidates; pairs ABSENT
   828	    from that cache (never-seen or previously negative, F06) are always
   829	    re-probed fresh regardless of TTL, so a transient failure only ever
   830	    costs one retry, never the whole cache window. `cache_write=False`
   831	    (SEC-A03: the simulate/explain lane) reads a warm cache but never
   832	    persists one, preserving explain's no-mutation contract.
   833	
   834	    ADR-0034 (AC-08): whenever a cache is even consulted, the authenticated-opencode-
   835	    provider set is re-read fresh (`_live_opencode_auth_signature`, one cheap credential-
   836	    only call, never itself cached) and folded into the lookup/write key -- a credential
   837	    change is caught immediately, never only after the TTL window; the cache on disk
   838	    still holds model LISTINGS only, never the raw signature.
   839	    """
   840	    now = time.time() if now is None else now
   841	    if pairs is not None:
   842	        selected = [pair for pair in pairs if pair in _PAIR_COMMANDS]
   843	        return _probe_pairs(config, selected, timeout)
   844	    all_pairs = list(_PAIR_COMMANDS)
   845	    use_cache = cache_root is not None and _validate_cache_dir(Path(cache_root))
   846	    auth_signature = _live_opencode_auth_signature(timeout) if use_cache else ""
   847	    key = _cache_key(config, auth_signature) if use_cache else None
   848	    cached = _read_probe_cache(cache_root, key, now, config) if (use_cache and not fresh) else None
   849	    if cached is not None:
   850	        missing = [pair for pair in all_pairs if pair not in cached]

   331	def _parse_pi_models(stdout: str, provider: str) -> set[str]:
   332	    """`pi --list-models` prints a `provider  model  ...` column table (header row first,
   333	    columns whitespace-separated); only rows whose first column matches `provider` are
   334	    read. openai-codex ids are catalog-IDENTITY (returned verbatim, spike-verified);
   335	    anthropic raw ids are translated through PI_MODEL_MAP so callers intersect against the
   336	    SAME short-name vocabulary every other pair uses — a raw Pi id never leaks through
   337	    untranslated. An unrecognized header shape fails closed (PROVIDER_UNAUTHENTICATED),
   338	    same discipline as the opencode parsers above."""
   339	    lines = _clean_lines(stdout)
   340	    if not lines or lines[0].split()[:2] != ["provider", "model"]:
   341	        raise RoutingError("PROVIDER_UNAUTHENTICATED")
   342	    raw = {parts[1] for line in lines[1:] if len(parts := line.split()) >= 2 and parts[0] == provider}
   343	    if provider == "openai-codex":
   344	        return raw
   345	    return {short for short, canonical in PI_MODEL_MAP.get(provider, {}).items() if canonical in raw}

   348	def pi_auth_provider_keys() -> frozenset[str]:
   349	    """Read-only key-SET of `~/.pi/agent/auth.json` (spike Q2): provider NAMES only, never
   350	    token values, never logged. Any surprise (missing file, symlink, foreign-owned file,
   351	    foreign shape, bad JSON) is an empty set, fail-closed — this never raises and the
   352	    caller never sees a credential value, only whether a provider name is present as a
   353	    key. P3-SEC-002 repair (022 PKG-3): the uid check was missing here even though
   354	    `_read_credential_json` (below) has always had it -- a file owned by another uid on
   355	    the same box could be read and its keys folded into a routing decision (the real
   356	    gate at `_probe_pairs`, `provider not in pi_auth_provider_keys()`) as if it were this
   357	    user's own credential state. Same discipline as `_read_credential_json` now: `lstat`,
   358	    never a symlink, a regular file, owned by THIS uid."""
   359	    path = Path.home() / ".pi/agent/auth.json"
   360	    try:
   361	        st = path.lstat()
   362	        if _stat.S_ISLNK(st.st_mode) or not _stat.S_ISREG(st.st_mode) or st.st_uid != os.getuid():
   363	            return frozenset()
   364	        doc = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
   365	    except (OSError, ValueError):
   366	        return frozenset()
   367	    if not isinstance(doc, dict):
   368	        return frozenset()
   369	    return frozenset(key for key in doc if isinstance(key, str))

   371	
   372	def _binary_signature(name: str) -> str:
   373	    """ADR-0034 (AC-08), generalized by ADR-0043 (022 PKG-3, AC-07): path + mtime of
   374	    `name`'s resolved binary on PATH right now -- swapping the binary (upgrade,
   375	    reinstall, a different shim) invalidates every existing cache entry without a
   376	    manual version bump. Originally scoped to `opencode` alone; AC-07 extends the same
   377	    argument to `codex` and `claude` too -- any of the three CLI binaries can change how
   378	    it reads/writes its OWN credential store on a swap, so each binary's signature is
   379	    folded into `_cache_key` alongside its runtime's credential-file signature (below).
   380	    No subprocess here, just a PATH lookup and a stat -- safe to call from a pure
   381	    hashing function. Unresolvable is a fixed per-name sentinel that simply never
   382	    equals a real signature, so the cache is skipped (fail-closed), never fabricated as
   383	    a match."""
   384	    path = shutil.which(name)
   385	    if not path:
   386	        return f"{name}:absent"
   387	    try:
   388	        mtime_ns = os.stat(path).st_mtime_ns
   389	    except OSError:
   390	        return f"{name}:absent"
   391	    return f"{name}:{path}:{mtime_ns}"
   392	
   393	
   394	# ------------------------------------------------------------------ ADR-0043 (022 PKG-3)
   395	# Per-runtime credential signatures, AC-07: closes "de-auth invisible for up to
   396	# PROBE_CACHE_TTL (300s) in 3 of 4 runtimes" -- `_cache_key` used to fold in ONLY
   397	# opencode's live subprocess-based auth signature (`_live_opencode_auth_signature`
   398	# above). Every signature below is `stat`/local-read only -- ZERO new subprocesses
   399	# (the one subprocess this whole cache-key computation ever pays stays
   400	# `_live_opencode_auth_signature`, called by the CALLER and passed in, never invoked
   401	# from here -- see `test_adr0043_ac07_cache_key_never_spawns_a_subprocess_for_the_new_
   402	# signatures` in tests/test_routing.py and evidence/P3-implementer.md's proof). Same
   403	# security discipline as `pi_auth_provider_keys` throughout (AC-09): `lstat` (never a
   404	# symlink), a regular file, this uid, ONLY named fields ever read -- never a whole-file
   405	# hash or mtime, because `~/.claude/.credentials.json` also holds an unrelated
   406	# `mcpOAuth` block (today a Vercel token, live-measured) that rotates on ITS OWN MCP
   407	# refresh cycle with zero relation to the Claude Code provider credential; hashing the
   408	# file or its mtime would rotate this signature on every MCP token refresh, defeating
   409	# the cache for a reason that has nothing to do with what this signature exists to
   410	# track. Every value is hashed before being folded into `_cache_key` -- never logged,
   411	# never placed in an envelope, never a raw credential value.
   412	
   413	
   414	def _read_credential_json(path: Path) -> dict | None:
   415	    """Shared fail-closed read for a local per-runtime credential file: `lstat` (never
   416	    a symlink), a regular file, owned by this uid, valid JSON, a dict -- ANY surprise is
   417	    `None`, never raised, same discipline as `pi_auth_provider_keys` above. The caller
   418	    reads only specific NAMED fields from the result; this function itself never
   419	    inspects field values, so widening what a signature reads is always a one-line,
   420	    auditable change at the call site, never here."""
   421	    try:
   422	        st = path.lstat()
   423	        if _stat.S_ISLNK(st.st_mode) or not _stat.S_ISREG(st.st_mode) or st.st_uid != os.getuid():
   424	            return None
   425	        doc = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
   426	    except (OSError, ValueError):
   427	        return None
   428	    return doc if isinstance(doc, dict) else None
   429	
   430	
   431	def _codex_auth_signature() -> str:
   432	    """AC-07/AC-09: codex's per-runtime signature -- `~/.codex/auth.json`, ONLY
   433	    `tokens.account_id` (real account identity; codex is the one runtime of the three
   434	    whose credential file carries one, ADR-0043) and whether `OPENAI_API_KEY` is
   435	    present (a boolean, never the key's own value). NEVER `tokens.access_token`/
   436	    `tokens.refresh_token`/`tokens.id_token`/`last_refresh` -- token material and its
   437	    timestamp, which rotate on every refresh (AC-08 property 1: a signature built from
   438	    a rotating field would miss the cache on every routine OAuth renewal). File absent,
   439	    foreign-owned, a symlink, or unreadable -- fail-closed to the empty string, which
   440	    never equals a real signature, so the cache is simply skipped (a fresh probe runs),
   441	    never fabricated as a match (AC-08 property 2: this is what makes a real logout/
   442	    delete visible immediately).
   443	
   444	    P3-SEC-001 repair (022 PKG-3, AC-09): a VALID JSON object that is missing `tokens`
   445	    or whose `tokens.account_id` isn't a string (corrupt file, foreign shape, a `{}`
   446	    written mid-write) must fail closed too, exactly like a missing/foreign-owned/
   447	    unreadable file already does via `_read_credential_json` -- shape and type of every
   448	    field this signature actually reads are validated BEFORE hashing, never after. Before
   449	    this fix, a missing/mistyped `tokens.account_id` silently defaulted to `""` and STILL
   450	    got folded into a hash (`account_id=|api_key_present=...`), producing a fixed
   451	    non-empty signature instead of the required empty one -- fail-open, because that
   452	    non-empty value looked exactly like a real (if account-id-less) signature to
   453	    `_cache_key`, so a corrupt-shaped file could still warm/serve a cache entry."""
   454	    doc = _read_credential_json(Path.home() / ".codex/auth.json")
   455	    if doc is None:
   456	        return ""
   457	    tokens = doc.get("tokens")
   458	    if not isinstance(tokens, dict):
   459	        return ""
   460	    account_id = tokens.get("account_id")
   461	    if not isinstance(account_id, str):
   462	        return ""
   463	    has_api_key = isinstance(doc.get("OPENAI_API_KEY"), str)
   464	    material = f"account_id={account_id}|api_key_present={has_api_key}"
   465	    return hashlib.sha256(material.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
   466	
   467	
   468	def _claude_code_auth_signature() -> str:
   469	    """AC-07/AC-09: claude-code's per-runtime signature -- `~/.claude/.credentials.json`,
   470	    ONLY the three `claudeAiOauth` fields measured NON-rotating across a refresh
   471	    (`scopes`, `subscriptionType`, `rateLimitTier` -- attributes of the OAuth grant/plan
   472	    itself, not of the token instance; evidence/P3-implementer.md). NEVER
   473	    `accessToken`/`refreshToken`/`expiresAt`/`refreshTokenExpiresAt` (token material,
   474	    AC-09: "hashear el refreshToken no se hace"), and NEVER the file's OTHER top-level
   475	    key `mcpOAuth` (the trap this module's own section docstring names).
   476	    ADR-0043's declared limit: `claudeAiOauth` carries NO account-identity field (unlike
   477	    codex's `tokens.account_id`) -- this signature therefore detects PRESENCE/ABSENCE
   478	    (logout, deletion, file absent) but never a same-plan account swap without an
   479	    intervening logout. Closing that gap would mean hashing `refreshToken`, which this
   480	    package explicitly does not do.
   481	
   482	    P3-SEC-001 repair (022 PKG-3, AC-09): `claudeAiOauth` being present as a dict is not
   483	    enough -- shape and type of the three fields this signature actually reads are
   484	    validated BEFORE hashing (`scopes` a list, `subscriptionType`/`rateLimitTier` a
   485	    string each); any absence or wrong type fails closed to the empty string. Before this
   486	    fix, `{"claudeAiOauth": {}}` (or any object missing one of the three fields) still
   487	    produced a fixed non-empty hash of the literal string `"None"` in place of each
   488	    missing field, indistinguishable from a real signature to `_cache_key` -- fail-open
   489	    on a corrupt/foreign-shaped file."""
   490	    doc = _read_credential_json(Path.home() / ".claude/.credentials.json")
   491	    if doc is None:
   492	        return ""
   493	    oauth = doc.get("claudeAiOauth")
   494	    if not isinstance(oauth, dict):
   495	        return ""
   496	    scopes, subscription_type, rate_limit_tier = (oauth.get(f) for f in
   497	                                                   ("scopes", "subscriptionType", "rateLimitTier"))
   498	    if not isinstance(scopes, list) or not isinstance(subscription_type, str) \
   499	            or not isinstance(rate_limit_tier, str):
   500	        return ""
   501	    parts = [str(scopes), subscription_type, rate_limit_tier]
   502	    return hashlib.sha256("|".join(parts).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
   503	
   504	
   505	def _pi_auth_signature() -> str:
   506	    """AC-07: pi's per-runtime signature folds `pi_auth_provider_keys()` (already
   507	    stat/local-read only, zero subprocess, spike Q2 discipline -- untouched by this
   508	    package beyond the uid check, P3-SEC-002) with `PI_PINNED_VERSION` (the EXACT pi
   509	    release the probe invokes, above) -- a version bump is itself credential-relevant: a
   510	    different pinned pi release could read/write a differently-shaped auth.json. Provider
   511	    NAMES only (never a token value), hashed before folding into `_cache_key`.
   512	
   513	    P3-SEC-002 repair (022 PKG-3, AC-09): an EMPTY key-set (file absent, symlink,
   514	    foreign-owned, corrupt/foreign-shaped JSON -- anything `pi_auth_provider_keys()`
   515	    itself already fails closed on) must fail closed HERE too, to the empty string, same
   516	    as `_codex_auth_signature`/`_claude_code_auth_signature` above. Before this fix, an
   517	    empty key-set was folded into a hash alongside `PI_PINNED_VERSION` regardless,
   518	    producing a fixed non-empty signature for "pi has no credentials" -- fail-OPEN,
   519	    because a non-empty signature is indistinguishable, at the `_cache_key` level, from a
   520	    real credential state. This is safe to change: `_pi_auth_signature`'s only consumer
   521	    is `_cache_key` (cache freshness), never the real per-pair authentication gate
   522	    (`_probe_pairs` calls `pi_auth_provider_keys()` directly, catalog.py:753) -- an empty
   523	    signature can only ever cost a cache miss (a fresh, correctly-gated probe runs next),
   524	    never fabricate or suppress which pairs are actually authenticated."""
   525	    keys = pi_auth_provider_keys()
   526	    if not keys:
   527	        return ""
   528	    names = ",".join(sorted(keys))
   529	    return hashlib.sha256(f"{names}|{PI_PINNED_VERSION}".encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
   530	
   531	
   532	def _live_opencode_auth_signature(timeout: float) -> str:
   533	    """ADR-0034 (AC-08): a cheap, ALWAYS-fresh (never itself cached) read of `opencode
   534	    auth list --pure` -- credential NAMES only, never a model listing, never persisted
   535	    to disk on its own -- folded into the cache key so the authenticated-provider set is
   536	    genuinely re-checked on every composition that consults the cache, instead of trusted
   537	    from whatever it was when the cache was last written. Any surprise (missing binary,
   538	    timeout, non-zero exit, parse failure) is the empty signature, fail-closed: an
   539	    unreadable auth state simply differs from a real one, so the cache is missed and a
   540	    full fresh probe runs -- it can never fabricate a match."""
   541	    probe_env = dict(os.environ, CI="1", NO_COLOR="1", TERM="dumb")
   542	    try:
   543	        completed = subprocess.run(("opencode", "auth", "list", "--pure"), stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
   544	                                   stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, text=True,
   545	                                   timeout=timeout, check=False, env=probe_env)
   760	            try:
   761	                provider_key = _OPENCODE_PROVIDER_KEYS[provider]
   762	                credentials = _parse_opencode_auth(auth_result.stdout)
   763	            except (RoutingError, ValueError, KeyError, IndexError, TypeError):
   764	                continue
   765	            if provider_key not in credentials:
   766	                continue
   767	            models_result = _run_cached(ran, commands[1], pair_timeout, probe_env)
   768	            if models_result is None or models_result.returncode != 0:
   769	                continue
   770	            try:
   771	                cli_id = _OPENCODE_CLI_IDS[provider]
   772	                parsed = _parse_opencode_models(models_result.stdout, cli_id)
   773	            except (RoutingError, ValueError, KeyError, IndexError, TypeError):
   774	                continue
   775	            # AC-05 layer 3 is unaffected by "auto": billing_rank's fail-closed-to-
   776	            # expensive default already applies to any provider PROVIDER_BILLING_KIND
   777	            # does not classify as "subscription" -- ceiling state never enters that map.
   778	            models = parsed if state == "auto" else (allowed & parsed)
   779	        else:
   780	            completed, failed = [], False
   781	            for argv in commands:
   782	                completed_item = _run_cached(ran, argv, pair_timeout, probe_env)
   783	                if completed_item is None:
   784	                    failed = True; break
   785	                completed.append(completed_item)
   786	            if failed or any(item.returncode != 0 for item in completed):
   787	                continue
   788	            try:
   789	                if runtime == "codex":
   790	                    # ADR-0042 AC-04: codex/claude-code never see state == "auto" here --
   791	                    # `models_config.load_config` dies if [catalog].codex/.claude are
   792	                    # absent or empty, so `allowed` is always the curated set by the time
   793	                    # a validated config reaches this function (see resolve_ceiling's
   794	                    # own docstring). Neither runtime has a models-LISTING command to
   795	                    # fall back on if it ever were "auto" (only a login-status boolean).
   796	                    models = allowed if _parse_codex_login(completed[0].stdout, completed[0].stderr) else set()
   797	                elif runtime == "claude-code":
   798	                    models = allowed if _parse_claude_auth(completed[0].stdout) else set()
   799	                else:  # pi
   800	                    # Belt-and-suspenders (T-305, spike Q2): the auth.json key-set is a cheap,
   801	                    # non-subprocess signal alongside the naturally fail-closed column-parse
   802	                    # below (an unauthenticated provider simply has no rows in --list-models).
   803	                    if provider not in pi_auth_provider_keys():
   804	                        continue
   805	                    # Same guarantee as codex/claude-code above: pi's two pairs are both
   806	                    # openai-codex/anthropic, always curated by validation.
   807	                    models = allowed & _parse_pi_models(completed[0].stdout, provider)
   808	            except (RoutingError, ValueError, KeyError, IndexError, TypeError):
   809	                continue
   810	        if models:
   811	            result[pair] = models
   812	    return result

   815	def probe_inventory(config: dict, timeout: float = 20.0, cache_root=None, fresh: bool = False,
   816	                    pairs=None, now=None, cache_write: bool = True) -> dict[tuple[str, str], set[str]]:
   817	    """Fresh exact pair-scoped observation with per-pair parsers.
   818	
   819	    Any surprise — missing binary, timeout, nonzero exit, unexpected shape,
   820	    error text — makes only that pair unavailable. Model names are always the
   821	    intersection with the canonical models.toml catalog for the provider, so a
   822	    runtime can never widen the audited model set. Raw provider output
   823	    (including account identifiers) is parsed and discarded, never returned.
   824	
   825	    `pairs` restricts probing to those exact pairs, always fresh and never
   826	    cached (the AM-2 fresh-selected path). Otherwise, with `cache_root`, a
   827	    valid unexpired cache (ADR-0006 key) filters candidates; pairs ABSENT
   828	    from that cache (never-seen or previously negative, F06) are always
   829	    re-probed fresh regardless of TTL, so a transient failure only ever
   830	    costs one retry, never the whole cache window. `cache_write=False`
   831	    (SEC-A03: the simulate/explain lane) reads a warm cache but never
   832	    persists one, preserving explain's no-mutation contract.
   833	
   834	    ADR-0034 (AC-08): whenever a cache is even consulted, the authenticated-opencode-
   835	    provider set is re-read fresh (`_live_opencode_auth_signature`, one cheap credential-
  3520	        # bypass upstream of build_snapshot -- a stale snapshot, a future code path that
  3521	        # skips it, or a curator error layer 1's normalization does not anticipate) must
  3522	        # still be denied at route-decide time by a dedicated hard exclusion, never merely
  3523	        # by the softer different-provider sort preference. Built directly against
  3524	        # RoutingService's hermetic test seam (`_for_tests`), which takes a snapshot
  3525	        # as-is and never re-derives it from build_snapshot -- exactly what makes this a
  3526	        # true "defense in depth" test, independent of layer 1.
  3527	        full_roles = ("implementer", "package-reviewer")
  3528	        tools = ("read", "shell", "write")
  3529	        writer_route = StaticRoute(2, "anthropic", "opus", "opus", "medium", "frontier", full_roles, tools, 20,
  3530	                                   StaticRoute.identifier(2, "anthropic", "opus", "opus", "medium", ("frontier",), full_roles, tools, 20))
  3531	        reviewer_route = StaticRoute(2, "opencode-zen", "claude-opus-4-8", "claude-opus", "medium", "frontier", full_roles, tools, 20,
  3532	                                     StaticRoute.identifier(2, "opencode-zen", "claude-opus-4-8", "claude-opus", "medium", ("frontier",), full_roles, tools, 20))
  3533	        identities = frozenset({
  3534	            (writer_route.route_id, "opencode", writer_route.provider, writer_route.model, writer_route.family, writer_route.effort),
  3535	            (reviewer_route.route_id, "opencode", reviewer_route.provider, reviewer_route.model, reviewer_route.family, reviewer_route.effort),
  3536	        })
  3537	        snapshot = CatalogSnapshot((writer_route, reviewer_route), identities)
  3538	        inventory = {("opencode", "anthropic"): {"opus"}, ("opencode", "opencode-zen"): {"claude-opus-4-8"}}
  3539	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
  3540	            svc = routing.RoutingService._for_tests(snapshot, self.roster, inventory, routing.RoutingStore._for_tests(Path(td) / "state"))
  3541	            writer_request = routing.TaskRequest("implementer", "change", "documentation", selected_runtime="opencode")
  3542	            writer_decision = svc.route(writer_request, self.observed(svc, "implementer", "opencode"))
  3543	            self.assertTrue(writer_decision.execution_enabled, writer_decision.reason_codes)
  3544	            self.assertEqual((writer_decision.provider, writer_decision.model), ("anthropic", "opus"))
  3545	            svc.store.mark_dispatched(writer_decision.run_id)
  3546	            svc.store.close_run(writer_decision.run_id, "success")
  3547	            review_request = routing.TaskRequest("package-reviewer", "inspection", "documentation", selected_runtime="opencode")
  3548	            review_facts = self.observed(svc, "package-reviewer", "opencode", operation="inspection", read_write="read")
  3549	            review = svc.route(review_request, review_facts, review_of_run_id=writer_decision.run_id)
  3550	        self.assertFalse(review.execution_enabled)
  3551	        self.assertEqual(review.reason_codes, ("REVIEWER_INDEPENDENCE_UNAVAILABLE",))
  3552	        self.assertFalse(review.independence_verified)
  3553	        self.assertIn({"route_id": reviewer_route.route_id, "reason": "REVIEW_MODEL_CONFLICT"}, review.exclusions)
  3554	
  3555	    def test_sec002_every_curated_anthropic_id_resolves_to_a_zen_curated_canonical_id(self):
  3556	        """Repair SEC-002 (delta-review round 2, medium). SEC-001's CANONICAL_MODEL was
  3557	        seeded ONLY from PI_MODEL_MAP -- which covers `opus`/`sonnet`/`haiku` because Pi
  3558	        happens to need those three translated, not because it is any kind of security
  3559	        curation -- leaving `fable` (the fourth id `[catalog].claude` curates, absent from
  3560	        PI_MODEL_MAP because Pi has no fable route) without a canonical alias at all, so
  3561	        `canonical_model('anthropic', 'fable')` fell back to identity and never collided
  3562	        with `canonical_model('opencode-zen', 'claude-fable-5')`: the exact SEC-001 hole,
  3563	        reopened for the one Anthropic model PI_MODEL_MAP doesn't translate.
  3564	
  3565	        This is the coherence check the panel asked for, generalized past `fable`
  3566	        specifically: EVERY id `[catalog].claude` curates today must resolve, through
  3567	        `canonical_model`, to a canonical id `[catalog].opencode_zen` actually curates --
  3568	        so a future fifth Anthropic id added to the allowlist without an explicit curated

  3575	        for short in claude_ids:

  3577	            self.assertIn(canonical, zen_ids, (short, canonical))

  3579	        # name, not only by the generalized loop above, so this test still fails on its own
  3580	        # even if a future edit narrowed `[catalog].claude` to no longer include it.
  3581	        self.assertIn("fable", claude_ids)
  3582	        self.assertEqual(cat.canonical_model("anthropic", "fable"), "claude-fable-5")
  3583	        self.assertEqual(cat.canonical_model("anthropic", "fable"),
  3584	                         cat.canonical_model("opencode-zen", "claude-fable-5"))

  3609	    def test_ac04_opencode_lane_providers_are_coherent_across_every_hardcoded_map(self):
  3610	        """Repair F-04 (012 repair, medium): a single declared provider set is checked for
  3611	        presence across every hardcoded map that must extend in lockstep whenever an
  3612	        OpenCode-lane provider is added -- catalog.py's _OPENCODE_PROVIDER_KEYS/
  3613	        _OPENCODE_CLI_IDS/PROVIDER_BILLING_KIND/_PAIR_COMMANDS, and models_config.py's
  3614	        optional [catalog] key validation (load_config) and preservation (emit). Also
  3615	        covers repair F-05: _PAIR_COMMANDS's opencode argv is DERIVED from
  3616	        _OPENCODE_CLI_IDS, not a second hardcoded copy -- asserted directly here so a
  3617	        future revert back to a literal duplicate is caught."""
  3618	        from routing_core import catalog as cat
  3619	        opencode_lane_providers = {"opencode-zen", "opencode-go"}
  3620	        self.assertEqual(opencode_lane_providers, set(cat._OPENCODE_CLI_IDS) - {"openai-codex", "anthropic"})
  3621	        for provider in opencode_lane_providers:
  3622	            self.assertIn(provider, cat._OPENCODE_PROVIDER_KEYS)
  3623	            self.assertIn(provider, cat._OPENCODE_CLI_IDS)
  3624	            self.assertIn(provider, cat.PROVIDER_BILLING_KIND)
  3625	            self.assertIn(("opencode", provider), cat._PAIR_COMMANDS)
  3626	            # F-05: the argv actually registered for this pair is EXACTLY what deriving it
  3627	            # from _OPENCODE_CLI_IDS would produce -- not an independently hand-typed copy.
  3628	            cli_id = cat._OPENCODE_CLI_IDS[provider]
  3629	            self.assertEqual(cat._PAIR_COMMANDS[("opencode", provider)],
  3630	                             (("opencode", "auth", "list", "--pure"), ("opencode", "models", cli_id, "--pure")))
  3631	        # models_config.py sites 4/5: each provider's [catalog] key survives a full
  3632	        # load -> emit -> load cycle, with its exact member set intact.
  3633	        toml_keys = {"opencode-zen": "opencode_zen", "opencode-go": "opencode_go"}
  3634	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
  3635	            path = Path(td) / "models.toml"
  3636	            path.write_text(models_config.emit(self.config))
  3637	            reloaded = models_config.load_config(path)
  3638	            for provider, key in toml_keys.items():
  3639	                self.assertIn(provider, opencode_lane_providers)
  3640	                self.assertIn(key, reloaded["catalog"])
  3641	                self.assertEqual(set(reloaded["catalog"][key]), set(self.config["catalog"][key]))

  3644	    # `[catalog]` ceiling -- `resolve_ceiling` replaces `_configured_models`.

  3646	    def test_adr0042_pkg2_ac04_resolve_ceiling_is_a_pure_tri_state_reflection_of_the_toml(self):
  3647	        """AC-04: `resolve_ceiling` replaces `_configured_models`, which collapsed two
  3648	        genuinely different TOML shapes ("no key at all" and "curated as an explicit
  3649	        empty list") into the identical `set()`. This exercises all three states plus
  3650	        the "no dedicated catalog key at all" edge that also resolves to auto."""
  3651	        from routing_core import catalog as cat
  3652	        self.assertEqual(cat.resolve_ceiling(self.config, "opencode-zen"),
  3653	                         ("curated", set(self.config["catalog"]["opencode_zen"])))
  3654	        self.assertEqual(cat.resolve_ceiling(self.config, "openai-codex"),
  3655	                         ("curated", set(self.config["catalog"]["codex"])))
  3656	        auto_cfg = json.loads(json.dumps(self.config)); del auto_cfg["catalog"]["opencode_zen"]
  3657	        self.assertEqual(cat.resolve_ceiling(auto_cfg, "opencode-zen"), ("auto", None))
  3658	        veto_cfg = json.loads(json.dumps(self.config)); veto_cfg["catalog"]["opencode_go"] = []
  3659	        self.assertEqual(cat.resolve_ceiling(veto_cfg, "opencode-go"), ("veto", set()))
  3660	        self.assertEqual(cat.resolve_ceiling(self.config, "totally-unregistered-provider"), ("auto", None))

  3662	    def test_adr0042_pkg2_ac04_probe_pairs_probes_a_provider_with_no_catalog_key_instead_of_skipping_it(self):
  3663	        """The defect this package exists to fix (context pack, verbatim): before this
  3664	        package, `_probe_pairs`'s `if not allowed: continue` skipped ANY provider absent
  3665	        from `[catalog]` entirely -- exactly what forced editing models.toml to add a
  3666	        provider. `auto_cfg` removes `[catalog].opencode_zen`; the SAME CLI stub output
  3667	        is probed once against `auto_cfg` (must surface, unfiltered) and once against
  3668	        the real curated `self.config` (must still be filtered out -- the model is not
  3669	        in the curated allowlist), proving the tri-state distinction end to end. Also
  3670	        covers AC-05 layer 1: a pair absent from `_PAIR_COMMANDS` is never probed no
  3671	        matter what ceiling state a (bogus) provider string would resolve to."""
  3672	        from routing_core import catalog as cat
  3673	        auto_cfg = json.loads(json.dumps(self.config)); del auto_cfg["catalog"]["opencode_zen"]
  3674	
  3675	        def fake_run(argv, **kwargs):
  3676	            if argv[1:4] == ("auth", "list", "--pure"):

  3687	    def test_adr0042_pkg2_ac04_read_probe_cache_auto_mode_never_goes_permanently_empty(self):

  3690	        intersects an "auto" provider's cached models against an EMPTY ceiling (there is

  3693	        cache document directly (bypassing any probe) for an auto-ceiling provider and
  3694	        asserts the read survives with its models intact, never silently emptied."""

52:  proveedores ya están auditados por otros runtimes. **pi nunca puede aportar un proveedor

101-### PKG-3 — `liveness-real`
102:- **AC-07**: el de-auth se cierra **en la clave de caché, no con más subprocesos**: firma de
103-  credencial por runtime, toda `stat`/lectura local — codex (`~/.codex/auth.json`), claude-code
104:  (`~/.claude/.credentials.json`), pi (`pi_auth_provider_keys()` + `PI_PINNED_VERSION`),
105-  opencode (lo de hoy + mtime de los binarios `codex` y `claude`).
106:- **AC-08**: **las dos propiedades que hacen o rompen esto**, cada una con su test:
107:  *refresh de token* (cambia sólo token/expiry) ⇒ la firma **no** cambia y el caché sirve; y
108:  *logout / credencial borrada* ⇒ la firma **sí** cambia ⇒ probe fresco. Sin la primera,
109:  Claude Code renueva OAuth y probeás en cada decisión, hasta 60 s en pi
110-  (`PI_PROBE_MIN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS`).
111:- **AC-09**: la firma se compone de **presencia e identidad de credencial, nunca material y
112-  nunca campos que rotan**, hasheada, jamás logueada ni en el envelope — misma disciplina que
113:  `pi_auth_provider_keys`. Bump de `_CACHE_SCHEMA_VERSION` con su test: hoy no existe ninguno.
114-  **Límite estructural de claude-code, aceptado por Federico y a documentar en ADR-0043** (F2):
115:  `~/.claude/.credentials.json` **no tiene ningún campo de identidad de cuenta** — codex sí
116-  (`tokens.account_id`), y los únicos campos no-rotantes de claude (`scopes`,
117-  `subscriptionType`, `rateLimitTier`) no identifican una cuenta. Así que para claude-code la
118:  firma detecta **presencia y ausencia** (logout, credencial borrada, archivo ausente) pero
119-  **no** un cambio de una cuenta a otra del mismo plan sin pasar por logout. Detectarlo exigiría
120-  hashear el `refreshToken`, que esta feature **no** hace. El límite se declara; no se disimula.
121-  **Supuesto a validar temprano en este paquete, no de memoria** (F2): que `refreshToken`,
122-  `scopes` y `subscriptionType` efectivamente no roten en un refresh normal. Captura A/B del
123:  archivo antes y después de un refresh real; si rotan, la firma de claude-code hay que
124-  rediseñarla.
125-- **AC-10**: **una sola caché**, en la raíz del store (`store.py:326-334`), que ya tiene la
126-  disciplina de directorio privado y es la que usa la vía de decisión. Migran
--
145-  **cualquier provider que el usuario haya agregado a mano como `origin=user`**. Desde el primer
146-  arranque se puede listar, verificar y quitar desde `set-agents`, que es el pedido literal.
147-  La alternativa —registrar solo lo que el harness reconoce— dejaba invisibles los providers
148:  propios de quien ya tenía el harness instalado, que es el caso más probable.
149-  **A nadie le desaparece nada**: el registro declara qué hay y de dónde vino, nunca borra.
150-
151-### PKG-5 — `altas-y-bajas-automaticas`
--
153-  intenta una **verificación empírica** con candidatos derivados **sólo del nombre de la
154-  credencial autenticada** (exacto; espacio→guion). El id se acepta **únicamente si el CLI
155-  contestó bien**: listado con prefijo `<id>/<model>` bien formado, parseado por
156:  `_parse_opencode_models`. No es heurística que adivina: es medición que confirma.
157-- **AC-17**: la baja es **simétrica y automática**. El registro es *memoria del CLI id*, nunca
158-  una autorización: la ruteabilidad siempre exige probe vivo. Con AC-07, se nota en la decisión
159-  siguiente.
--
174-## No-goals
175-
176-- No se toca el sort key (`service.py:382`) ni se le agrega un factor. El consumo es 023.
177:- No se agregan filas curadas a `routes.v1.toml` para proveedores nuevos, Copilot incluido:
178-  entran por la vía sintetizada o no entran.
179-- No se probea dentro de `route()`, por decisión ni por spawn.
180-- No se inventan CLI ids ni se derivan por regla.
181-- No se edita ADR-0034 retroactivamente: se supersede en parte y `docs/adr/README.md` marca la
182-  relación.
183:- No se convierte a pi en descubridor de credenciales: se declara el límite.
184-
185-## Riesgos
186-
187-1. **El techo auto infla el pool** (60 modelos zen sintetizados) → sort más lento. Mitigación:
188-   cap por proveedor, `is_inferred` ya penaliza, y medir p50/p90 antes y después.
189:2. **La firma rota en cada refresh** → probe en cada decisión, hasta 60 s en pi. Mitigación: la
190:   firma excluye campos que rotan, y el test de propiedad refresh-vs-logout es obligatorio.
191-3. **Leer archivos de credencial es superficie sensible nueva.** Mitigación: misma disciplina
192:   que `pi_auth_provider_keys` — lstat, regular, uid propio, sólo nombres, hash antes de
193-   guardar, nunca en log ni envelope.
194-4. **La poda de subárboles JSON borra una clave del usuario.** Mitigación: sólo ids registrados
195-   en el manifiesto, con test de un provider hecho a mano presente.
--
206-## Criterio de cierre
207-
208-Desvincular una credencial y que la **decisión siguiente** ya no la ofrezca (hoy tarda hasta
209:300 s en 3 de 4 runtimes). Agregar un proveedor propio desde `set-agents`, usarlo, **quitarlo**,
210-y que un install posterior **no lo reponga**. Y `--route-doctor` reportando sobre la caché que
211-el decisor realmente usa.

import json, os, tempfile
from pathlib import Path
from unittest import mock
import sys
sys.path.insert(0, 'ai/scripts')
from routing_core import catalog as cat

VALID_CODEX = {\"tokens\": {\"account_id\": \"fixture-account\"}}
VALID_CLAUDE = {\"claudeAiOauth\": {\"scopes\": [\"fixture:scope\"], \"subscriptionType\": \"fixture-plan\", \"rateLimitTier\": \"fixture-tier\"}}
VALID_PI = {\"openai-codex\": {\"apiKey\": \"fixture-placeholder\"}}

def write(path, obj=None, raw=None):
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path.write_text(raw if raw is not None else json.dumps(obj), encoding='utf-8')
    os.chmod(path, 0o600)

def call(kind):
    return {\"codex\": cat._codex_auth_signature, \"claude\": cat._claude_code_auth_signature, \"pi\": cat._pi_auth_signature}[kind]()

            (\"top-level-nonobject\", []), (\"empty-file\", None), (\"symlink\", \"SYMLINK\"), (\"foreign-uid\", VALID_CODEX),
        ],
        \"claude\": [
            (\"empty-object\", {}), (\"missing-oauth\", {\"other\": 1}),
            (\"oauth-wrong-type\", {\"claudeAiOauth\": []}),
            (\"scopes-wrong-type\", {\"claudeAiOauth\": {\"scopes\": \"bad\", \"subscriptionType\": \"p\", \"rateLimitTier\": \"t\"}}),
            (\"subscription-wrong-type\", {\"claudeAiOauth\": {\"scopes\": [], \"subscriptionType\": 1, \"rateLimitTier\": \"t\"}}),
            (\"tier-wrong-type\", {\"claudeAiOauth\": {\"scopes\": [], \"subscriptionType\": \"p\", \"rateLimitTier\": 1}}),
            (\"top-level-nonobject\", []), (\"empty-file\", None), (\"symlink\", \"SYMLINK\"), (\"foreign-uid\", VALID_CLAUDE),

            (\"provider-value-wrong-type\", {\"openai-codex\": []}),
        ],

    for kind, items in cases.items():
        p = paths[kind]
        for label, obj in items:
            p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            if p.exists() or p.is_symlink(): p.unlink()
            if obj == \"SYMLINK\":
                target = home / f'{kind}-target.json'
                write(target, VALID_CODEX if kind == 'codex' else VALID_CLAUDE if kind == 'claude' else VALID_PI)
                p.symlink_to(target)
                foreign = False
            elif obj is None:
                write(p, raw='')
                foreign = False
            else:
                write(p, obj)
                foreign = label == 'foreign-uid'
            with mock.patch.object(cat.Path, 'home', return_value=home):
                if foreign:
                    with mock.patch.object(cat.os, 'getuid', return_value=os.getuid() + 1):
                        empty = call(kind) == ''
                else:
                    empty = call(kind) == ''
            print(f'{kind}:{label}: empty={empty}')

        write(p, VALID_CODEX if kind == 'codex' else VALID_CLAUDE if kind == 'claude' else VALID_PI)
        with mock.patch.object(cat.Path, 'home', return_value=home):
            print(f'{kind}:valid: nonempty={bool(call(kind))}')

  4407	    def test_adr0034_ac02_ac06_discovered_reviewer_resolves_reviewer_independence_unavailable(self):
  4408	        # F-01 (P1 repair): AC-02/AC-06's own headline benefit -- "a scenario that today
  4409	        # returns REVIEWER_INDEPENDENCE_UNAVAILABLE resolves once a discovered provider is
  4410	        # available" -- was never pinned by any test, leaving a future refactor of the
  4411	        # exclusion loop or the sort key free to silently kill the package's whole reason
  4412	        # for existing with the suite still green. Reproduces the live case measured
  4413	        # during implementation: writer `openai-codex/gpt-5.6-sol`, the only CURATED
  4414	        # reviewer candidate conflicts on PROVIDER (`openai-codex/gpt-5.6-luna`, same
  4415	        # provider as the writer) so, pre-discovery, this decision is exactly the
  4416	        # `REVIEWER_INDEPENDENCE_UNAVAILABLE` halt the live log showed 9 times. With
  4417	        # discovery, an INFERRED `opencode-zen/kimi-k3` reviewer is selected (resolvable,
  4418	        # distinct vendor stem) and its own inferred sibling `opencode-zen/gpt-5.1-codex`
  4419	        # (same `gpt` stem as the writer) is excluded.
  4420	        from routing_core.domain import CatalogSnapshot, StaticRoute
  4421	        full_roles = ("implementer", "package-reviewer")
  4422	        tools = ("read", "shell", "write")
  4423	
  4424	        def route(provider, model, family, priority):
  4425	            return StaticRoute(2, provider, model, family, "medium", "balanced", full_roles, tools, priority,
  4426	                                StaticRoute.identifier(2, provider, model, family, "medium", ("balanced",),
  4427	                                                        full_roles, tools, priority))
  4428	
  4429	        writer_route = route("openai-codex", "gpt-5.6-sol", "gpt-5.6", 10)
  4430	        curated_conflict_route = route("openai-codex", "gpt-5.6-luna", "gpt-5.6", 5)  # same provider -> hard-excluded
  4431	        inferred_kimi_route = route("opencode-zen", "kimi-k3", "kimi-k3", 1000)  # distinct, resolvable stem
  4432	        inferred_gpt_route = route("opencode-zen", "gpt-5.1-codex", "gpt-5.1-codex", 1000)  # same stem as writer
  4433	
  4434	        def identities_for(r):
  4435	            return {(r.route_id, rt, r.provider, r.model, r.family, r.effort) for rt in ("codex", "opencode")}
  4436	
  4437	        identities = frozenset().union(*(identities_for(r) for r in
  4438	                                          (writer_route, curated_conflict_route, inferred_kimi_route, inferred_gpt_route)))
  4439	        snapshot = CatalogSnapshot((writer_route, curated_conflict_route, inferred_kimi_route, inferred_gpt_route), identities)
  4440	        inventory = {("codex", "openai-codex"): {"gpt-5.6-sol"},
  4441	                     ("opencode", "openai-codex"): {"gpt-5.6-sol", "gpt-5.6-luna"},
  4442	                     ("opencode", "opencode-zen"): {"kimi-k3", "gpt-5.1-codex"}}
  4443	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
  4444	            # Without discovery (no inferred candidates at all), the sole curated
  4445	            # reviewer candidate is provider-conflicted -- proving the pre-package halt
  4446	            # this scenario actually reproduces.
  4447	            curated_only_snapshot = CatalogSnapshot((writer_route, curated_conflict_route), identities)
  4448	            curated_only_svc = routing.RoutingService._for_tests(
  4449	                curated_only_snapshot, self.roster, inventory, routing.RoutingStore._for_tests(Path(td) / "curated-only"))
  4450	            writer_decision = curated_only_svc.route(
  4451	                routing.TaskRequest("implementer", "change", "documentation", selected_runtime="codex"),
  4452	                self.observed(curated_only_svc, "implementer", "codex"))
  4453	            self.assertTrue(writer_decision.execution_enabled, writer_decision.reason_codes)
  4454	            curated_only_svc.store.mark_dispatched(writer_decision.run_id)
  4455	            curated_only_svc.store.close_run(writer_decision.run_id, "success")
  4456	            pre_discovery_review = curated_only_svc.route(
  4457	                routing.TaskRequest("package-reviewer", "inspection", "documentation", selected_runtime="opencode"),
  4458	                self.observed(curated_only_svc, "package-reviewer", "opencode", operation="inspection", read_write="read"),
  4459	                review_of_run_id=writer_decision.run_id)
  4460	            self.assertFalse(pre_discovery_review.execution_enabled)
  4461	            self.assertEqual(pre_discovery_review.reason_codes, ("REVIEWER_INDEPENDENCE_UNAVAILABLE",))
  4462	
  4463	            # Now the discovered candidates are on the table: the same writer decision
  4464	            # resolves to a selected, independence-verified, inferred reviewer.
  4465	            svc = routing.RoutingService._for_tests(snapshot, self.roster, inventory, routing.RoutingStore._for_tests(Path(td) / "discovered"))
  4466	            svc._inferred_ids = frozenset({inferred_kimi_route.route_id, inferred_gpt_route.route_id})
  4467	            writer_decision2 = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest("implementer", "change", "documentation", selected_runtime="codex"),
  4468	                                         self.observed(svc, "implementer", "codex"))
  4469	            self.assertTrue(writer_decision2.execution_enabled, writer_decision2.reason_codes)
  4470	            svc.store.mark_dispatched(writer_decision2.run_id)
  4471	            svc.store.close_run(writer_decision2.run_id, "success")
  4472	            review_request = routing.TaskRequest("package-reviewer", "inspection", "documentation", selected_runtime="opencode")
  4473	            review_facts = self.observed(svc, "package-reviewer", "opencode", operation="inspection", read_write="read")
  4474	            review = svc.route(review_request, review_facts, review_of_run_id=writer_decision2.run_id)
  4475	        self.assertFalse(review.execution_enabled)  # reviews never set execution_enabled
  4476	        self.assertTrue(review.independence_verified, review.reason_codes)
  4477	        self.assertEqual((review.provider, review.model), ("opencode-zen", "kimi-k3"))
  4478	        self.assertIn("MODEL_METADATA_INFERRED tier=balanced family=kimi-k3", review.reason_codes)
  4479	        self.assertIn({"route_id": curated_conflict_route.route_id, "reason": "REVIEW_FAMILY_CONFLICT"}, review.exclusions)
  4480	        self.assertIn({"route_id": inferred_gpt_route.route_id, "reason": "REVIEW_FAMILY_CONFLICT"}, review.exclusions)
