  4520	    def test_adr0034_ac08_reprobe_rejection_reranks_to_the_next_candidate(self):
  4521	        # AC-08: the top-ranked candidate failing its FRESH reprobe used to abort the
  4522	        # whole decision with PROVIDER_UNAUTHENTICATED; it now retries against the next
  4523	        # already-filtered candidate, only exhausting to PROVIDER_UNAUTHENTICATED when
  4524	        # NONE survive.
  4525	        inv = {("codex", "openai-codex"): {"gpt-5.6-luna", "gpt-5.6-sol"}}
  4526	        rejected = {"gpt-5.6-luna"}  # the higher-ranked candidate (lower curated_priority wins ties;
  4527	                                      # both are "fast"-eligible here, tier/bias/curated_priority order it first)
  4528	
  4529	        def flaky_reprobe(pairs):
  4530	            return {pair: {m for m in inv.get(pair, set()) if m not in rejected} for pair in pairs}
  4531	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
  4532	            svc = self.service(Path(td) / "s", inventory=inv, reprobe=flaky_reprobe)
  4533	            decision = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest("implementer", "change", "mechanical", selected_runtime="codex"),
  4534	                                 self.observed(svc, "implementer", "codex", task_class="mechanical", context_required=False))
  4535	            self.assertTrue(decision.execution_enabled, decision.reason_codes)
  4536	            self.assertEqual(decision.model, "gpt-5.6-sol")  # the rejected candidate was skipped, not aborted on
  4537	            svc.store.abandon(decision.run_id)
  4538	
  4539	        def all_rejecting_reprobe(pairs):
  4540	            return {pair: set() for pair in pairs}
  4541	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
  4542	            svc = self.service(Path(td) / "s", inventory=inv, reprobe=all_rejecting_reprobe)
  4543	            decision = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest("implementer", "change", "mechanical", selected_runtime="codex"),
  4544	                                 self.observed(svc, "implementer", "codex", task_class="mechanical", context_required=False))
  4545	            self.assertFalse(decision.execution_enabled)
  4546	            self.assertEqual(decision.reason_codes, ("PROVIDER_UNAUTHENTICATED",))
  4547	            self.assertEqual(svc.store.open_runs(), [])  # nothing durably authorized on exhaustion
  4548	
  4549	    def test_adr0034_f04_repair_reprobe_rejection_leaves_an_exclusion_trace(self):
  4550	        # F-04 (P1 repair): the AC-08 re-rank above used to drop the reprobe-rejected top
  4551	        # candidate WITHOUT a trace -- no exclusion entry, no reason code -- so the
  4552	        # winning decision (a candidate that was NOT top-ranked) showed up in
  4553	        # decisions-v1.jsonl with nothing explaining why. This is purely additive: the
  4554	        # winning decision's own identity/runtime/success are unchanged from the AC-08
  4555	        # test above, and the codes REPAIR added never replace or reorder existing ones.
  4556	        inv = {("codex", "openai-codex"): {"gpt-5.6-luna", "gpt-5.6-sol"}}
  4557	        rejected = {"gpt-5.6-luna"}
  4558	
  4559	        def flaky_reprobe(pairs):
  4560	            return {pair: {m for m in inv.get(pair, set()) if m not in rejected} for pair in pairs}
  4561	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
  4562	            svc = self.service(Path(td) / "s", inventory=inv, reprobe=flaky_reprobe)
  4563	            rejected_route = next(r for r in svc.snapshot.routes if r.provider == "openai-codex" and r.model == "gpt-5.6-luna")
  4564	            decision = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest("implementer", "change", "mechanical", selected_runtime="codex"),
  4565	                                 self.observed(svc, "implementer", "codex", task_class="mechanical", context_required=False))

  4567	            self.assertEqual(decision.model, "gpt-5.6-sol")
  4568	            self.assertIn({"route_id": rejected_route.route_id, "reason": "REPROBE_REJECTED openai-codex/gpt-5.6-luna"},
  4569	                           decision.exclusions)
  4570	            svc.store.abandon(decision.run_id)

  4587	    def test_adr0034_f05_repair_effective_providers_short_circuits_when_base_covers_discoverable(self):
  4588	        # F-05 (P1 repair): `_MODEL_PREFERENCE_PROVIDERS` already covers
  4589	        # `models_config.DISCOVERABLE_PROVIDERS` today (AC-10's own lockstep test), so the
  4590	        # union in `_effective_preference_providers` is a structural no-op -- yet it used
  4591	        # to pay a live `probe_inventory` subprocess probe (each `_PAIR_COMMANDS` entry
  4592	        # can block up to 20s) on every call regardless. The cheap set-comparison
  4593	        # short-circuit must skip the probe entirely on this (today's real) branch.
  4594	        import set_agents_app as app
  4595	        with mock.patch("routing_core.catalog.probe_inventory", return_value={}) as probe:
  4596	            self.assertEqual(app._effective_preference_providers(), app._MODEL_PREFERENCE_PROVIDERS)
  4597	            probe.assert_not_called()
  4598	        # Only once the audited set genuinely grows past the base set does the probe run
  4599	        # again -- proving the short-circuit is a real conditional, not a permanent skip.
  4600	        with mock.patch.object(app.models_config, "DISCOVERABLE_PROVIDERS",
  4601	                                frozenset({*app._MODEL_PREFERENCE_PROVIDERS, "a-future-only-provider"})):
  4602	            with mock.patch("routing_core.catalog.probe_inventory", return_value={}) as probe:
  4603	                with mock.patch("routing_core.catalog.resolve_discovered_providers", return_value=()):
  4604	                    app._effective_preference_providers()
  4605	            probe.assert_called_once()
  4606	
  4607	    def test_adr0034_m1_github_copilot_never_gets_an_audited_pair_even_authenticated(self):
  4608	        # M-1 (ADR-0034): github-copilot is authenticated in the live measurement but
  4609	        # unlistable -- no pair exists for it in `_PAIR_COMMANDS` at all, so "auto" can
  4610	        # never adopt it no matter what a (hypothetical, malformed) inventory claims.
  4611	        self.assertNotIn(("opencode", "github-copilot"), routing_catalog._PAIR_COMMANDS)
  4612	        config = json.loads(json.dumps(self.config))
  4613	        config["routing"]["discovered_providers"] = "auto"
  4614	        # Even if something upstream ever produced an inventory entry for it (never
  4615	        # possible through the real probe, which only iterates `_PAIR_COMMANDS`), the
  4616	        # derivation intersects against the audited provider set and drops it.
  4617	        bogus_inventory = {("opencode", "github-copilot"): {"some-copilot-model"}}
  4618	        self.assertEqual(routing_catalog.resolve_discovered_providers(config, bogus_inventory), ())

  4656	    def test_adr0042_ac01b_model_preference_providers_is_guarded_against_the_real_source(self):
  4657	        # AC-01b: before this ADR, the ONLY assertion touching `_MODEL_PREFERENCE_PROVIDERS`
  4658	        # lived at test_adr0034_ac09_write_cli_validates_pins_against_the_effective_set_
  4659	        # not_just_the_constant (below, :3965 pre-refactor line count) -- inside a
  4660	        # `mock.patch` block whose real purpose is testing the live-probe degrade path, and
  4661	        # it compared against ANOTHER hardcoded literal, never against the audited source.
  4662	        # That test is untouched by this package (still passes, proven by the full-suite
  4663	        # gate) and still is not the guard -- THIS is the guard: two independent
  4664	        # cross-checks against the real source, neither of them a second copy-pasted tuple.
  4665	        #
  4666	        # Cross-check 1: same pattern as test_adr0034_ac10_discoverable_providers_lockstep_
  4667	        # guard above (which only ever covered DISCOVERABLE_PROVIDERS, never this tuple,
  4668	        # despite set_agents_app.py's own comment claiming otherwise pre-ADR-0042) --
  4669	        # membership against `_PAIR_COMMANDS`'s own audited provider set.
  4670	        pair_providers = {provider for _, provider in routing_catalog._PAIR_COMMANDS}
  4671	        self.assertEqual(set(set_agents_app._MODEL_PREFERENCE_PROVIDERS), pair_providers)
  4672	        # Cross-check 2 (P1 repair, finding F01): comparing against `tuple(provider_
  4673	        # registry.PROVIDERS)` directly, as this test did before the repair, WAS the
  4674	        # false-green this package exists to close -- both sides re-derive from the SAME
  4675	        # dict, so renaming, adding, removing, or reordering a `PROVIDERS` entry moves them
  4676	        # together and the assertion stays green no matter what. The independent check is a
  4677	        # hand-pinned literal of the audited universe instead, deliberately NOT imported or
  4678	        # derived from `provider_registry`: a TEST is allowed to hardcode the universe it
  4679	        # audits (that is its job -- fail noisily the moment someone changes it, on purpose
  4680	        # or not); only PRODUCTION code must never hand-maintain a second copy. Do not "fix"
  4681	        # this by deriving the literal below from PROVIDERS again.
  4682	        self.assertEqual(set_agents_app._MODEL_PREFERENCE_PROVIDERS,
  4683	                          ("openai-codex", "anthropic", "opencode-zen", "opencode-go"))

  4713	                                     "architect", "agent-factory", "ux-ui-designer", "package-planner"})

  4715	                                  "security-auditor", "finding-verifier", "adversarial-judge"})

import json, os, sys, tempfile, types
from pathlib import Path
from unittest import mock
sys.path.insert(0, 'ai/scripts')
from routing_core import catalog as cat

with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
    home = Path(td)
    auth = home / '.pi/agent/auth.json'
    auth.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
    auth.write_text(json.dumps({'openai-codex': {'apiKey': 'fixture-placeholder'}}), encoding='utf-8')
    os.chmod(auth, 0o600)
    def fake_run(argv, **kwargs):
        return types.SimpleNamespace(returncode=0, stdout='provider model\\nopenai-codex gpt-5.6-luna\\n', stderr='')
    with mock.patch.object(cat.Path, 'home', return_value=home), \\
         mock.patch.object(cat.subprocess, 'run', side_effect=fake_run), \\
         mock.patch.object(cat, '_pi_auth_signature', return_value=''):
        result = cat.probe_inventory({'catalog': {'codex': ['gpt-5.6-luna'], 'claude': ['opus'], 'opencode_zen': ['z'], 'opencode_go': ['g']}, 'routes': []}, pairs=[('pi', 'openai-codex')], timeout=5.0)
        print('valid-owned-keyset=', cat.pi_auth_provider_keys() == frozenset({'openai-codex'}))
        print('valid-owned-pair-probed=', result.get(('pi', 'openai-codex')) == {'gpt-5.6-luna'})
    with mock.patch.object(cat.Path, 'home', return_value=home), \\
         mock.patch.object(cat.os, 'getuid', return_value=os.getuid() + 1), \\
         mock.patch.object(cat.subprocess, 'run', side_effect=fake_run):
        result = cat.probe_inventory({'catalog': {'codex': ['gpt-5.6-luna'], 'claude': ['opus'], 'opencode_zen': ['z'], 'opencode_go': ['g']}, 'routes': []}, pairs=[('pi', 'openai-codex')], timeout=5.0)
        print('foreign-uid-keyset-empty=', cat.pi_auth_provider_keys() == frozenset())
        print('foreign-uid-pair-absent=', ('pi', 'openai-codex') not in result)
PY

VEREDICTO: revision_required

Hallazgo:
ID: P3-DR-F01
SEVERIDAD: critical
DONDE: ai/scripts/routing_core/catalog.py:369
QUE: `pi_auth_provider_keys()` acepta un objeto malformado con proveedor conocido y valor inválido (por ejemplo, una lista). Devuelve la clave, `_pi_auth_signature()` queda no vacía y `_probe_pairs()` puede atravesar el gate por membresía. Viola AC-09 fail-closed. Es una cuarta guarda falsa-verde: el test llama “foreign JSON shape” a su cobertura, pero sólo prueba JSON top-level lista, no la forma objeto inválida.
PRUEBA:

Comando literal:
```text

...
PY
```

Salida literal:
```text
pi_known_provider_value_list_signature_empty= False
pi_known_provider_value_list_keyset_empty= False
```

Reparación mínima: validar y filtrar las entradas de Pi para los proveedores auditados (`openai-codex`, `anthropic`), exigiendo que su valor sea un objeto; un archivo sin proveedor auditado válido debe devolver conjunto y firma vacíos. Agregar regresión para `{"openai-codex":[]}` y para un objeto sólo con proveedor no auditado.
